- Widow's Wail. I like that.

227
00:16:58,592 --> 00:17:00,626
Every time I use it,
it'll be like cutting off

228
00:17:00,660 --> 00:17:02,294
Ned Stark's head
all over again.

229
00:17:30,740 --> 00:17:32,541
Don't.

230
00:17:34,578 --> 00:17:36,629
You want me on the desk?

231
00:17:38,748 --> 00:17:42,301
- <i> What's wrong, my lion?</i>
- Don't call me that.

232
00:17:42,335 --> 00:17:45,004
What should I call you?

233
00:17:45,055 --> 00:17:48,007
I'm afraid our friendship
can't continue.

234
00:17:48,041 --> 00:17:49,809
Our friendship?

235
00:17:49,843 --> 00:17:52,178
There's a ship waiting in
the harbor bound for Pentos.

236
00:17:53,263 --> 00:17:54,430
What?

237
00:17:54,481 --> 00:17:55,898
You'll have your own
cabin, of course,

238
00:17:55,932 --> 00:18:00,319
and across the Narrow Sea
a house, servants.

239
00:18:01,571 --> 00:18:03,022
What is this?

240
00:18:03,056 --> 00:18:05,241
I'm a married man.

241
00:18:05,275 --> 00:18:08,160
My wife has suffered a great
deal, as you well know.

242
00:18:08,195 --> 00:18:11,330
<i>I don't want her to suffer
anymore on my account.</i>

243
00:18:11,364 --> 00:18:13,532
I need to uphold my vows.

244
00:18:13,566 --> 00:18:16,202
She doesn't want you.

245
00:18:16,236 --> 00:18:18,871
- <i> I need to do right by her.</i>
- You don't want her.

246
00:18:18,905 --> 00:18:20,589
By our children.

247
00:18:22,876 --> 00:18:24,960
- What are you afraid of?
- I'm not afraid.

248
00:18:25,011 --> 00:18:28,413
You are. You are afraid of
your father and your sister.

249
00:18:28,431 --> 00:18:31,112
- You're going to run from them all your life?
- I need you to leave.

250
00:18:31,134 --> 00:18:34,136
I'm not afraid of them.
I'm not going to run.

251
00:18:34,187 --> 00:18:36,522
- Shae.
- We will fight them together.

252
00:18:36,556 --> 00:18:38,924
It's like you said,
I am yours and you are mine.

253
00:18:38,942 --> 00:18:41,060
You're a whore!

254
00:18:43,396 --> 00:18:46,282
Sansa is fit to bear
my children and you are not.

255
00:18:46,316 --> 00:18:48,784
I can't be in love
with a whore.

256
00:18:48,818 --> 00:18:51,237
I can't have children
with a whore.

257
00:18:51,271 --> 00:18:53,956
How many men
have you been with?

258
00:18:53,990 --> 00:18:56,242
500? 5,000?

259
00:19:02,115 --> 00:19:04,533
How many whores
have you been with?

260
00:19:04,568 --> 00:19:06,719
I have enjoyed my time
with all of them

261
00:19:06,753 --> 00:19:08,837
and I have enjoyed my time
with you most of all.

262
00:19:08,889 --> 00:19:11,173
But now that time is over.

263
00:19:26,940 --> 00:19:30,159
You'll have a comfortable
life in Pentos.

264
00:19:32,162 --> 00:19:35,030
Bronn will escort you
to your ship.

265
00:20:00,506 --> 00:20:03,025
Hear us now.

266
00:20:03,059 --> 00:20:05,644
Accept these tokens
of our faith, my lord,

267
00:20:05,678 --> 00:20:07,613
and lead us from the darkness.

268
00:20:09,199 --> 00:20:11,867
Sire, I served you well.

269
00:20:11,901 --> 00:20:14,820
<i>Lord of Light,</i>
<i>show us the way...</i>

270
00:20:14,871 --> 00:20:19,291
Selyse, you're my sister!

271
00:20:19,326 --> 00:20:20,859
Yours are the stars
that guide us.

272
00:20:20,877 --> 00:20:23,495
Tell him!
Tell him!

273
00:20:23,663 --> 00:20:25,714
<i>Lord of Light, protect us,</i>

274
00:20:25,749 --> 00:20:27,499
for the night is dark
and full of terrors.

275
00:20:39,646 --> 00:20:42,097
Did you see?
Their souls.

276
00:20:42,148 --> 00:20:44,566
It was their souls.

277
00:20:44,601 --> 00:20:47,653
Our lord took them.
Did you see?

278
00:20:54,227 --> 00:20:57,229
Lord Florent was
your brother by law.

279
00:20:57,247 --> 00:20:59,531
He was an infidel.

280
00:20:59,565 --> 00:21:02,034
He worshipped the gods
of his fathers

281
00:21:02,068 --> 00:21:04,236
and their fathers before him.

282
00:21:04,254 --> 00:21:06,905
They were the gods
of your fathers, too.

283
00:21:06,923 --> 00:21:09,241
I ordered him to tear
down his idols.

284
00:21:09,259 --> 00:21:10,709
He disobeyed.

285
00:21:10,743 --> 00:21:13,045
How many ships did he
bring to your cause?

286
00:21:13,079 --> 00:21:14,346
How many men?

287
00:21:14,381 --> 00:21:16,715
A good deal more than you.

288
00:21:18,184 --> 00:21:20,019
Did you see, Ser Davos?

289
00:21:20,053 --> 00:21:21,353
They're with our lord now.

290
00:21:21,388 --> 00:21:23,922
Their sins all burned away.
Did you see?

291
00:21:23,940 --> 00:21:26,608
I'm sure they're more
than grateful, my queen.

292
00:21:40,907 --> 00:21:42,658
Meat's off.

293
00:21:44,077 --> 00:21:46,445
Our larders are almost empty.

294
00:21:46,463 --> 00:21:48,497
Serve fish, then.
We're on an island.

295
00:21:48,548 --> 00:21:50,115
You hate fish.

296
00:21:50,133 --> 00:21:52,918
I hate a good many things,
but I suffer them all the same.

297
00:21:54,471 --> 00:21:58,340
When Storm's End was under
siege and I was starving,

298
00:21:58,391 --> 00:22:02,478
<i>Stannis boiled soup</i>
<i>for me from books.</i>

299
00:22:02,512 --> 00:22:05,597
The binding glue
is made from horses.

300
00:22:08,518 --> 00:22:13,155
One morning, he shot two
seagulls on the beach.

301
00:22:13,189 --> 00:22:16,859
I've never tasted anything
as good as grilled seagull.

302
00:22:18,111 --> 00:22:20,612
- Do you remember?
- Of course I remember.

303
00:22:23,316 --> 00:22:25,918
Have you ever known
true hunger, my lady?

304
00:22:25,952 --> 00:22:28,153
<i>It's all I knew</i>
<i>when I was a child.</i>

305
00:22:28,171 --> 00:22:30,339
Until you found
the Lord of Light?

306
00:22:30,373 --> 00:22:32,925
Until he found me.

307
00:22:39,832 --> 00:22:42,050
I fear for our daughter's soul.

308
00:22:43,770 --> 00:22:45,637
<i>Every mother should fear</i>
<i>for her child's soul.</i>

309
00:22:45,671 --> 00:22:47,806
- She's a stubborn little beast.
- She's a child.

310
00:22:47,840 --> 00:22:49,942
You barely know her.

311
00:22:49,976 --> 00:22:52,844
You think she's sweet because
she smiles when you visit.

312
00:22:52,862 --> 00:22:55,147
She's sullen and stubborn
and sinful.

313
00:22:55,181 --> 00:22:58,200
Why else would the Lord of Light
have seen fit to mark her face?

314
00:22:58,234 --> 00:22:59,785
She needs the rod.

315
00:22:59,819 --> 00:23:03,288
She's my daughter.
You will not strike her.

316
00:23:04,657 --> 00:23:06,325
As you command.

317
00:23:08,411 --> 00:23:10,829
Perhaps the Lady Melisandre
could speak with her.

318
00:23:18,588 --> 00:23:20,222
Come in.

319
00:23:22,392 --> 00:23:24,676
Were you sleeping, Princess?

320
00:23:29,315 --> 00:23:31,683
Did you watch the ceremony
on the beach?

321
00:23:31,717 --> 00:23:34,102
I heard it.

322
00:23:34,154 --> 00:23:35,737
And it frightened you?

323
00:23:35,772 --> 00:23:37,656
Ser Axel was my uncle.

324
00:23:37,690 --> 00:23:39,942
<i>He was always kind to me.</i>

325
00:23:39,993 --> 00:23:41,944
They're in a better
place now, Princess.

326
00:23:41,995 --> 00:23:44,830
<i>The fire cleansed them</i>
<i>of the sins of the world.</i>

327
00:23:44,864 --> 00:23:46,448
But they screamed.

328
00:23:46,499 --> 00:23:48,500
Women scream
when they give birth.

329
00:23:48,535 --> 00:23:51,119
Afterward they are
filled with joy.

330
00:23:51,171 --> 00:23:52,838
Afterward they aren't
ash and bone.

331
00:23:52,872 --> 00:23:54,873
You have so many
questions, don't you?

332
00:23:54,907 --> 00:23:57,092
<i>So did I when I was a child.</i>

333
00:23:57,126 --> 00:23:59,461
I was much like you.

334
00:23:59,512 --> 00:24:02,097
Only I wasn't a princess.

335
00:24:02,131 --> 00:24:03,715
And you didn't have this.

336
00:24:03,749 --> 00:24:05,601
No.

337
00:24:05,635 --> 00:24:08,554
But I suffered in other ways,
sweet girl, believe me.

338
00:24:08,588 --> 00:24:10,973
What do you know of the gods?

339
00:24:11,024 --> 00:24:13,275
I read
"The Seven-Pointed Star."

340
00:24:13,309 --> 00:24:16,094
Lies and fables.

341
00:24:16,112 --> 00:24:19,147
Septons speak of seven gods.

342
00:24:19,199 --> 00:24:20,732
There are but two...

343
00:24:20,783 --> 00:24:23,151
a god of light and love and joy

344
00:24:23,203 --> 00:24:26,822
and a god of darkness,
evil, and fear.

345
00:24:26,873 --> 00:24:28,607
Eternally at war.

346
00:24:28,625 --> 00:24:32,628
There are no seven heavens
and no seven hells?

347
00:24:33,946 --> 00:24:36,415
There's only one hell,
Princess.

348
00:24:36,449 --> 00:24:39,618
The one we live in now.

349
00:25:13,870 --> 00:25:16,788
<i>Hodor.</i>

350
00:25:20,877 --> 00:25:22,127
Why did you wake me?

351
00:25:22,161 --> 00:25:25,097
You've been gone for hours.

352
00:25:25,131 --> 00:25:26,765
I was hungry.

353
00:25:26,799 --> 00:25:28,850
We're all hungry.

354
00:25:28,885 --> 00:25:30,602
I was just eating.

355
00:25:30,637 --> 00:25:33,639
Summer was eating.

356
00:25:33,723 --> 00:25:36,642
Your body can't live on
the food your wolf consumes.

357
00:25:36,693 --> 00:25:39,811
<i>Spending too much time in
Summer's skin is dangerous.</i>

358
00:25:41,397 --> 00:25:43,198
You're not a direwolf, Bran.

359
00:25:46,235 --> 00:25:48,186
Must be glorious, though.

360
00:25:48,204 --> 00:25:51,873
To run, to leap, to hunt.

361
00:25:53,042 --> 00:25:54,893
To be whole.

362
00:25:54,928 --> 00:25:57,696
<i>I know that's tempting,</i>

363
00:25:57,714 --> 00:25:59,998
but if you're trapped
in Summer for too long,

364
00:26:00,032 --> 00:26:01,883
you'll forget what it was
to be human.

365
00:26:05,872 --> 00:26:08,006
You'd forget us, Bran.

366
00:26:08,040 --> 00:26:09,925
You'd forget your mother
and father.

367
00:26:09,976 --> 00:26:11,977
You'd forget your brothers
and sisters.

368
00:26:12,011 --> 00:26:13,729
You'd forget Winterfell.

369
00:26:13,763 --> 00:26:15,681
You'd forget you.

370
00:26:15,715 --> 00:26:19,851
And if we lose you,
we lose everything.

371
00:26:51,351 --> 00:26:54,553
Hodor, take me to the tree.

372
00:27:42,802 --> 00:27:44,770
<i>Look for me...</i>

373
00:27:50,193 --> 00:27:51,777
<i>beneath the tree.</i>

374
00:27:55,698 --> 00:27:57,982
<i>He saw us.</i>

375
00:28:02,088 --> 00:28:03,288
<i>North.</i>

376
00:28:12,682 --> 00:28:14,966
I know where we have to go.

377
00:29:02,515 --> 00:29:05,400
Let it be known
that Margaery of House Tyrell

378
00:29:05,434 --> 00:29:09,321
<i>and Joffrey of the Houses</i>
<i>Lannister and Baratheon</i>

379
00:29:09,355 --> 00:29:13,191
are one heart, one flesh,
one soul.

380
00:29:13,225 --> 00:29:15,861
<i>Cursed be he</i>

381
00:29:15,895 --> 00:29:19,114
<i>who would seek</i>
<i>to tear them asunder.</i>

382
00:29:21,200 --> 00:29:25,587
With this kiss,
I pledge my love.

383
00:29:46,943 --> 00:29:49,027
We have a new queen.

384
00:29:50,980 --> 00:29:52,948
Better her than you.

385
00:30:05,144 --> 00:30:08,079
- Bit much, wouldn't you say?
- It feels proportionate.

386
00:30:08,113 --> 00:30:11,249
- Proportionate to what?
- The expected extravagance.

387
00:30:11,334 --> 00:30:13,718
People who spend their money
on this sort of nonsense

388
00:30:13,753 --> 00:30:15,337
tend not to have it for long.

389
00:30:15,388 --> 00:30:18,590
You ought to try enjoying
something before you die.

390
00:30:18,624 --> 00:30:20,175
You might find it suits you.

391
00:30:20,226 --> 00:30:23,845
Not now, Mace. Lord Tywin
and I are speaking.

392
00:30:23,896 --> 00:30:26,314
Anyway, I don't know what
you're complaining about.

393
00:30:26,349 --> 00:30:27,732
I'm paying my share.

394
00:30:27,767 --> 00:30:30,068
Shall we stick golden roses
in half the meat pies

395
00:30:30,102 --> 00:30:31,770
to commemorate your generosity?

396
00:30:31,804 --> 00:30:35,273
No, your heartfelt thank-you
is its own reward.

397
00:30:35,307 --> 00:30:38,276
I would imagine I'd be hearing
it again before long.

398
00:30:38,311 --> 00:30:40,478
Wars are rather expensive.

399
00:30:40,496 --> 00:30:43,498
"The Iron Bank
will have its due."

400
00:30:43,532 --> 00:30:46,284
How they love
to remind everyone.

401
00:30:46,318 --> 00:30:48,987
Almost as much as you
Lannisters with your debts.

402
00:30:49,005 --> 00:30:50,972
I'm not worried about
the Iron Bank.

403
00:30:51,007 --> 00:30:53,592
We both know
you're smarter than that.

404
00:30:53,626 --> 00:30:57,712
Come, Tywin, let us
celebrate young love.

405
00:31:09,609 --> 00:31:11,443
All taken care of.

406
00:31:11,477 --> 00:31:14,396
- You saw her board the ship?
- Aye, she's on it.

407
00:31:14,447 --> 00:31:16,314
You saw the ship sail away?

408
00:31:16,348 --> 00:31:19,985
No one knows she's there
but you, me, and Varys.

409
00:31:20,019 --> 00:31:21,903
How do you know?

410
00:31:21,954 --> 00:31:24,489
Because if someone follows
me without an invitation,

411
00:31:24,523 --> 00:31:26,241
I'm the last person
they ever follow.

412
00:31:26,292 --> 00:31:28,376
There's someone following you?

413
00:31:30,162 --> 00:31:31,796
She's gone.

414
00:31:31,831 --> 00:31:34,165
I know you don't want to
believe it, but she is.

415
00:31:34,199 --> 00:31:37,502
Now, go drink till it feels
like you did the right thing.

416
00:31:43,559 --> 00:31:45,727
Hello.

417
00:31:45,761 --> 00:31:48,263
- Hello.
- Not you.

418
00:32:04,447 --> 00:32:06,564
You look exquisite, child.

419
00:32:06,582 --> 00:32:09,901
The wind has been
at you, though.

420
00:32:09,919 --> 00:32:13,038
I haven't had the
opportunity to tell you

421
00:32:13,072 --> 00:32:15,840
how sorry I was to hear
about your brother.

422
00:32:15,875 --> 00:32:19,377
War is war, but killing
a man at a wedding,

423
00:32:19,428 --> 00:32:21,012
horrid.

424
00:32:21,047 --> 00:32:23,264
What sort of monster
would do such a thing?

425
00:32:23,299 --> 00:32:26,768
As if men need more
reasons to fear marriage.

426
00:32:26,802 --> 00:32:28,303
My lady.
My lady.

427
00:32:28,354 --> 00:32:31,606
Lord Tyrion, you see?
Not as bad as all that.

428
00:32:31,640 --> 00:32:33,308
Perhaps if your pauper husband

429
00:32:33,359 --> 00:32:35,894
were to sell his mule
and his last pair of shoes,

430
00:32:35,928 --> 00:32:39,230
he might be able to afford to bring
you to Highgarden for a visit.

431
00:32:39,264 --> 00:32:42,283
Now that peace has come and
all is right with the world,

432
00:32:42,368 --> 00:32:44,402
it would do you good
to see some of it.

433
00:32:44,436 --> 00:32:47,405
You must excuse me.
It's time I ate

434
00:32:47,439 --> 00:32:49,541
some of this food I paid for.

435
00:32:53,212 --> 00:32:56,081
♪ A coat of gold ♪

436
00:32:56,115 --> 00:32:58,783
♪ Or a coat of red ♪

437
00:32:58,801 --> 00:33:04,672
<i>♪ A lion still has claws ♪</i>

438
00:33:04,724 --> 00:33:07,142
♪ And mine,
my lord... ♪

439
00:33:07,176 --> 00:33:09,627
Very good. Very good.
Off you go.

440
00:33:26,362 --> 00:33:29,080
My love, why don't we
make the announcement?

441
00:33:31,650 --> 00:33:33,201
Everyone.

442
00:33:33,252 --> 00:33:37,038
The queen would like
to say a few words.

443
00:33:44,597 --> 00:33:46,431
We are so fortunate

444
00:33:46,465 --> 00:33:48,683
to enjoy this marvelous
food and drink.

445
00:33:48,717 --> 00:33:51,219
Not all among us are so lucky.

446
00:33:51,270 --> 00:33:55,190
To thank the gods for bringing
the recent war to a just end,

447
00:33:55,224 --> 00:33:58,526
King Joffrey has decreed
that the leftovers

448
00:33:58,561 --> 00:34:02,280
from our feast be given
to the poorest in his city.

449
00:34:13,959 --> 00:34:16,161
You're an example to us all.

450
00:34:32,428 --> 00:34:34,429
Ser Jaime, I'm very sorry.

451
00:34:34,480 --> 00:34:36,147
Ser Loras,
it's quite all right.

452
00:34:38,934 --> 00:34:40,885
Your sister looks
very beautiful.

453
00:34:40,903 --> 00:34:42,437
<i>As does yours.</i>

454
00:34:42,488 --> 00:34:45,556
So, looking forward
to your wedding?

455
00:34:45,574 --> 00:34:47,909
Yes, very much.

456
00:34:47,943 --> 00:34:50,328
Our fathers are both
rather keen on the prospect.

457
00:34:50,362 --> 00:34:51,863
They certainly are.

458
00:34:51,897 --> 00:34:53,164
Perhaps they should
get married.

459
00:34:55,367 --> 00:34:57,785
If you were to marry Cersei,

460
00:34:57,837 --> 00:34:59,420
she'd murder you in your sleep.

461
00:34:59,455 --> 00:35:03,174
If you somehow managed
to put a child in her first,

462
00:35:03,209 --> 00:35:05,343
she'd murder him, too,

463
00:35:05,377 --> 00:35:07,078
long before he drew
his first breath.

464
00:35:07,096 --> 00:35:10,215
Luckily for you,
none of this will happen

465
00:35:10,249 --> 00:35:12,851
because you'll never marry her.

466
00:35:15,721 --> 00:35:17,805
And neither will you.

467
00:35:31,370 --> 00:35:33,488
Your Grace.
My king.

468
00:35:33,522 --> 00:35:35,406
- My queen.
- Lady Brienne.

469
00:35:35,440 --> 00:35:37,709
So good of you to come.

470
00:35:37,743 --> 00:35:39,160
I'm no lady, Your Grace.

471
00:35:39,211 --> 00:35:41,612
Did you just bow?

472
00:35:41,630 --> 00:35:43,114
Apologies, Your Grace.

473
00:35:43,132 --> 00:35:45,884
I never did master the curtsy.

474
00:35:45,918 --> 00:35:47,502
You're the one who put a sword
through Renly Baratheon.

475
00:35:47,553 --> 00:35:50,505
That's not true, my love. Brienne
had nothing to do with it.

476
00:35:50,556 --> 00:35:52,257
A shame.

477
00:35:52,291 --> 00:35:54,926
I'd knight the man that put an
end to that deviant's life.

478
00:35:56,095 --> 00:35:58,229
I just wanted
to congratulate you both

479
00:35:58,264 --> 00:35:59,614
and wish you good fortune.

480
00:35:59,648 --> 00:36:01,566
The country has been
at war too long.

481
00:36:01,600 --> 00:36:04,819
I hope your reign
is long and peaceful.

482
00:36:04,853 --> 00:36:06,671
- Yes, yes.
- Thank you.

483
00:36:06,705 --> 00:36:08,890
I hope we see more of you.

484
00:36:15,698 --> 00:36:17,749
Lady Brienne.

485
00:36:17,783 --> 00:36:20,618
You're Lord Selwyn
Tarth's daughter.

486
00:36:20,652 --> 00:36:23,621
That makes you a lady whether
you want to be or not.

487
00:36:23,655 --> 00:36:25,924
As you say, Your Grace.

488
00:36:25,958 --> 00:36:27,375
I owe you my gratitude.

489
00:36:27,426 --> 00:36:29,711
You returned my brother
safely to King's Landing.

490
00:36:32,181 --> 00:36:34,549
In truth, he rescued me,
Your Grace.

491
00:36:34,600 --> 00:36:36,317
More than once.

492
00:36:36,352 --> 00:36:38,469
Did he?

493
00:36:39,521 --> 00:36:41,139
Haven't heard
that story before.

494
00:36:42,725 --> 00:36:45,343
Not such a fascinating
story, I'm afraid.

495
00:36:45,361 --> 00:36:47,729
I'm sure you have many
fascinating stories.

496
00:36:47,780 --> 00:36:51,883
Sworn to Renly Baratheon. Sworn to Catelyn Stark.
And now my brother.

497
00:36:51,917 --> 00:36:55,403
Must be exciting to flit
from one camp to the next

498
00:36:55,454 --> 00:36:58,206
serving whichever lord
or lady you fancy.

499
00:36:58,240 --> 00:37:00,792
I don't serve
your brother, Your Grace.

500
00:37:00,826 --> 00:37:02,961
But you love him.

501
00:37:11,003 --> 00:37:12,920
Your Grace.

502
00:37:23,482 --> 00:37:27,518
No, no, come to my chambers and
I will examine you personally.

503
00:37:27,552 --> 00:37:29,821
- She'll do no such thing.
- Oh, Your Grace.

504
00:37:29,855 --> 00:37:32,023
Yes, well, this young
lady sought my advice...

505
00:37:32,057 --> 00:37:33,741
You should see Qyburn.
He's quite good.

506
00:37:33,776 --> 00:37:36,894
- Your Grace.
- Qyburn? Deplorable man.

507
00:37:36,912 --> 00:37:40,331
Brought shame on the Citadel
with his repugnant experiments.

508
00:37:40,366 --> 00:37:42,700
More repugnant than your gnarled
fingers on that girl's thighs?

509
00:37:42,734 --> 00:37:45,086
Your Grace,
I am a man of learning.

510
00:37:45,120 --> 00:37:47,922
My little brother had you sent to the
Black Cells when you annoyed him.

511
00:37:47,956 --> 00:37:50,041
What do you think I could do
to you if you annoyed me?

512
00:37:50,075 --> 00:37:52,043
I never meant to annoy anyone.

513
00:37:52,077 --> 00:37:54,962
But you are.
You annoy me right now.

514
00:37:55,014 --> 00:37:57,181
Every breath you draw
in my presence annoys me.

515
00:37:57,216 --> 00:37:58,716
So here's what I want
you to do.

516
00:37:58,750 --> 00:38:00,268
I want you to leave
my presence.

517
00:38:00,302 --> 00:38:01,752
Leave this wedding right now.

518
00:38:01,770 --> 00:38:03,421
Go to the kitchens
and instruct them

519
00:38:03,439 --> 00:38:06,891
that all the leftovers from the feast
will be brought to the kennels.

520
00:38:06,925 --> 00:38:09,143
Your Grace, Queen Margaery...

521
00:38:09,194 --> 00:38:11,529
The<i> queen</i> is telling you

522
00:38:11,563 --> 00:38:13,815
the leftovers
will feed the dogs

523
00:38:13,866 --> 00:38:16,067
or you will.

524
00:38:34,920 --> 00:38:37,955
A gold dragon to whoever
knocks my fool's hat off.

525
00:38:49,568 --> 00:38:51,769
You're in rather a good mood.

526
00:38:51,803 --> 00:38:54,105
- I suppose I am.
- I won't ask why.

527
00:38:54,139 --> 00:38:57,325
- Small pleasures.
- Your Grace. Lord Tywin.

528
00:38:57,359 --> 00:38:58,910
Prince Oberyn.

529
00:38:58,944 --> 00:39:00,278
I don't believe you
have met Ellaria.

530
00:39:00,312 --> 00:39:02,029
This is the Lord Hand
Tywin Lannister

531
00:39:02,081 --> 00:39:04,482
and Cersei Lannister
the Queen Regent.

532
00:39:04,500 --> 00:39:07,201
I suppose it is
former Queen Regent now.

533
00:39:07,252 --> 00:39:10,654
Lord Hand and Lady Cersei,

534
00:39:10,672 --> 00:39:14,709
- Ellaria Sand.
- My lord. My lady.

535
00:39:14,760 --> 00:39:16,494
Charmed.

536
00:39:16,512 --> 00:39:18,679
Can't say I've ever
met a Sand before.

537
00:39:22,551 --> 00:39:24,051
We are everywhere in Dorne.

538
00:39:24,136 --> 00:39:26,437
I have 10,000 brothers
and sisters.

539
00:39:26,472 --> 00:39:28,890
Bastards are born
of passion, aren't they?

540
00:39:28,941 --> 00:39:30,341
We don't despise them in Dorne.

541
00:39:30,359 --> 00:39:31,559
No?
How tolerant of you.

542
00:39:31,610 --> 00:39:33,644
I expect it is a relief,
Lady Cersei,

543
00:39:33,678 --> 00:39:36,364
giving up your regal
responsibilities.

544
00:39:36,398 --> 00:39:39,534
Wearing the crown for so many years
must have left your neck a bit crooked.

545
00:39:39,568 --> 00:39:41,569
I suppose you'll never
know, Prince Oberyn.

546
00:39:41,620 --> 00:39:43,538
It's a shame your older brother
couldn't attend the wedding.

547
00:39:43,572 --> 00:39:45,907
Please give him our regards.

548
00:39:45,958 --> 00:39:47,691
With any luck,
the gout will abate

549
00:39:47,709 --> 00:39:49,360
with time and he will
be able to walk again.

550
00:39:49,378 --> 00:39:51,295
They call it
the rich man's disease.

551
00:39:51,330 --> 00:39:52,696
A wonder you don't have it.

552
00:39:52,714 --> 00:39:54,582
Noblemen in my part
of the country

553
00:39:54,633 --> 00:39:57,702
don't enjoy the same lifestyle
as our counterparts in Dorne.

554
00:39:57,736 --> 00:39:59,587
People everywhere
have their differences.

555
00:39:59,638 --> 00:40:02,840
In some places the highborn
frown upon those of low birth.

556
00:40:02,874 --> 00:40:06,177
In other places the rape and
murder of women and children

557
00:40:06,211 --> 00:40:08,146
is considered distasteful.

558
00:40:10,098 --> 00:40:13,401
What a fortunate thing for
you, former Queen Regent,

559
00:40:13,435 --> 00:40:15,403
that your daughter
Myrcella has been sent

560
00:40:15,437 --> 00:40:17,772
to live in the latter
sort of place.

561
00:40:21,109 --> 00:40:24,912
Everyone, silence!
Clear the floor.

562
00:40:27,282 --> 00:40:31,068
There's been too much
amusement here today.

563
00:40:31,086 --> 00:40:34,922
A royal wedding
is not an amusement.

564
00:40:34,957 --> 00:40:37,925
A royal wedding is history.

565
00:40:37,960 --> 00:40:40,461
The time has come for all of us

566
00:40:40,512 --> 00:40:42,930
to contemplate our history.

567
00:40:43,932 --> 00:40:45,299
My lords...

568
00:40:47,886 --> 00:40:49,687
my ladies...

569
00:40:50,889 --> 00:40:53,007
I give you

570
00:40:53,041 --> 00:40:55,526
King Joffrey...

571
00:40:55,561 --> 00:40:58,029
<i>Renly, Stannis,</i>

572
00:40:58,063 --> 00:41:01,449
<i>Robb Stark, Balon Greyjoy.</i>

573
00:41:01,483 --> 00:41:04,785
The War of the Five Kings.

574
00:41:08,156 --> 00:41:10,291
I'm the rightful king.

575
00:41:11,460 --> 00:41:14,245
- King in the North!
- Yes!

576
00:41:18,383 --> 00:41:21,385
<i>Traitor. You're a traitor.</i>

577
00:41:21,420 --> 00:41:24,672
<i>For the Seven Kingdoms!</i>

578
00:41:28,460 --> 00:41:30,561
Let the war begin.

579
00:41:32,898 --> 00:41:35,600
Renly, you're no king.

580
00:41:35,634 --> 00:41:38,853
Away, degenerate.
Away. Away.

581
00:41:38,904 --> 00:41:41,522
Ooh, careful.

582
00:41:41,573 --> 00:41:44,075
- Go on.
- I want you to be my prince.

583
00:41:52,034 --> 00:41:53,584
Stannis!

584
00:41:53,619 --> 00:41:56,053
Who's got the gold now, Stark?

585
00:41:56,088 --> 00:41:59,423
I am the rightful king.
Challenge me, ruffian.

586
00:41:59,458 --> 00:42:01,259
- Take that.
- I'm drowning. I'm drowning.

587
00:42:01,293 --> 00:42:03,711
I am the King in the North!

588
00:42:07,883 --> 00:42:09,884
Not wildfire!

589
00:42:17,359 --> 00:42:20,111
Pay each of them 20 gold
when this is done.

590
00:42:20,145 --> 00:42:22,146
<i>Yes, my lord.</i>

591
00:42:22,180 --> 00:42:24,398
We'll have to find another
way to thank the king.

592
00:42:28,654 --> 00:42:30,571
Charge!

593
00:42:35,294 --> 00:42:37,795
Ready, again.

594
00:42:41,550 --> 00:42:43,384
I am the King in the North!

595
00:42:45,203 --> 00:42:46,971
- Charge!
- Charge!

596
00:42:47,005 --> 00:42:48,872
Yes!

597
00:42:48,890 --> 00:42:52,176
<i>Your head!</i>

598
00:43:26,678 --> 00:43:29,580
Fine gentlemen.

599
00:43:37,856 --> 00:43:40,307
Well fought.
Well fought.

600
00:43:40,359 --> 00:43:43,227
Here you are.
Champion's purse.

601
00:43:44,529 --> 00:43:48,816
Though you're not
the champion yet, are you?

602
00:43:48,867 --> 00:43:52,453
A true champion defeats
all the challengers.

603
00:43:52,487 --> 00:43:54,872
Surely there are
others out there

604
00:43:54,906 --> 00:43:57,491
who still dare
to challenge my reign.

605
00:43:57,542 --> 00:43:59,610
Uncle.

606
00:43:59,628 --> 00:44:02,880
How about you? I'm sure
they have a spare costume.

607
00:44:08,920 --> 00:44:11,972
One taste of combat was
enough for me, Your Grace.

608
00:44:12,007 --> 00:44:14,625
I would like to keep
what remains of my face.

609
00:44:14,643 --> 00:44:17,094
<i>I think you should fight him.</i>

610
00:44:17,128 --> 00:44:18,929
This was but a poor imitation

611
00:44:18,963 --> 00:44:20,898
of your own bravery
on the field of battle.

612
00:44:20,932 --> 00:44:23,434
I speak as a firsthand witness.

613
00:44:23,468 --> 00:44:25,486
<i>Climb down from the high table</i>

614
00:44:25,520 --> 00:44:27,605
<i>with your new Valyrian sword</i>

615
00:44:27,639 --> 00:44:30,808
and show everyone how
a true king wins his throne.

616
00:44:30,826 --> 00:44:33,160
<i>Be careful, though.</i>

617
00:44:33,195 --> 00:44:36,247
This one is clearly
mad with lust.

618
00:44:36,281 --> 00:44:40,117
It would be a tragedy for
the king to lose his virtue

619
00:44:40,168 --> 00:44:42,203
hours before his wedding night.

620
00:45:12,901 --> 00:45:14,735
A fine vintage.

621
00:45:14,786 --> 00:45:16,520
Shame that it spilled.

622
00:45:16,538 --> 00:45:18,456
It did not spill.

623
00:45:18,490 --> 00:45:20,741
My love, come back to me.

624
00:45:20,792 --> 00:45:22,543
It's time for
my father's toast.

625
00:45:24,329 --> 00:45:27,164
Well, how does he expect me
to toast without wine?

626
00:45:29,234 --> 00:45:33,721
Uncle, you can be my cupbearer

627
00:45:33,755 --> 00:45:36,006
seeing as you're
too cowardly to fight.

628
00:45:36,057 --> 00:45:38,259
Your Grace does me
a great honor.

629
00:45:38,310 --> 00:45:41,679
It's not meant as an honor.

630
00:46:13,011 --> 00:46:14,545
Bring me my goblet.

631
00:46:37,118 --> 00:46:39,486
What good is an empty cup?

632
00:46:40,572 --> 00:46:42,039
Fill it.

633
00:46:55,971 --> 00:46:57,171
Kneel.

634
00:46:59,507 --> 00:47:01,926
Kneel before your king.

635
00:47:06,598 --> 00:47:08,315
Kneel.

636
00:47:14,639 --> 00:47:17,307
I said...

637
00:47:17,325 --> 00:47:19,193
kneel!

638
00:47:22,614 --> 00:47:24,181
Look, the pie.

639
00:47:38,713 --> 00:47:40,130
My queen.

640
00:47:51,610 --> 00:47:54,111
Wonderful.

641
00:47:55,513 --> 00:47:58,382
Wonderful.

642
00:47:58,416 --> 00:48:00,284
<i>My hero.</i>

643
00:48:03,405 --> 00:48:06,540
- Can we leave now?
- Let's find out.

644
00:48:15,166 --> 00:48:16,383
Uncle.

645
00:48:19,838 --> 00:48:21,338
Where are you going?

646
00:48:21,372 --> 00:48:23,891
<i>You're my cupbearer, remember?</i>

647
00:48:23,925 --> 00:48:26,510
I thought I might change out of
these wet clothes, Your Grace.

648
00:48:26,544 --> 00:48:29,179
No, no, no. No, you're
perfect the way you are.

649
00:48:30,348 --> 00:48:32,599
Serve me my wine.

650
00:48:37,689 --> 00:48:40,574
Well, hurry up.
This pie is dry.

651
00:48:48,566 --> 00:48:50,367
Mm, good.

652
00:48:50,418 --> 00:48:52,786
<i>Needs washing down.</i>

653
00:48:52,837 --> 00:48:55,372
If it please Your Grace,
Lady Sansa is very tired.

654
00:48:55,406 --> 00:48:58,125
<i>No.</i>

655
00:48:58,176 --> 00:48:59,910
No, you'll wait here...

656
00:49:02,246 --> 00:49:05,466
<i>un...</i>

657
00:49:05,517 --> 00:49:07,551
Your Grace?

658
00:49:09,304 --> 00:49:11,939
It's nothing.

659
00:49:17,479 --> 00:49:20,064
- He's choking!
- Help the poor boy.

660
00:49:23,651 --> 00:49:26,537
- Idiots, help your king.
- Move away!

661
00:49:28,606 --> 00:49:31,492
- Joffrey! Joffrey!
- Help him!

662
00:49:31,543 --> 00:49:33,911
- Someone help him!
- Joffrey!

663
00:49:35,714 --> 00:49:38,465
- Come with me now.
<i>- Joffrey! Joffrey!</i>

664
00:49:38,500 --> 00:49:40,718
If you want to live,
we have to leave.

665
00:49:40,752 --> 00:49:42,052
Don't touch him!

666
00:49:48,176 --> 00:49:50,094
Joffrey.

667
00:49:50,128 --> 00:49:52,596
Please, Joffrey.

668
00:49:52,630 --> 00:49:56,183
Joffrey, what is it?

669
00:49:56,234 --> 00:49:58,652
Help him!

670
00:50:30,935 --> 00:50:33,387
My son.

671
00:50:39,060 --> 00:50:42,980
<i>He's gone.</i>
<i>Our king is gone.</i>

672
00:50:43,848 --> 00:50:45,199
He did this.

673
00:50:47,735 --> 00:50:50,954
He poisoned my son,

674
00:50:50,989 --> 00:50:52,656
your king.

675
00:50:52,690 --> 00:50:55,325
Take him.
Take him!

676
00:50:56,244 --> 00:50:58,045
Take him!

677
00:50:58,079 --> 00:51:00,214
Take him!

678
00:51:25,056 --> 00:51:28,425
<i>♪ In a coat of gold ♪</i>

679
00:51:28,460 --> 00:51:31,428
<i>♪ Or a coat of red ♪</i>

680
00:51:31,463 --> 00:51:37,067
<i>♪ A lion still has claws ♪</i>

681
00:51:37,101 --> 00:51:40,504
<i>♪ And mine are long ♪</i>

682
00:51:40,538 --> 00:51:43,507
<i>♪ And sharp, my lord ♪</i>

683
00:51:43,541 --> 00:51:49,546
<i>♪ As long and sharp as yours ♪</i>

684
00:51:49,581 --> 00:51:55,719
<i>♪ And so he spoke</i>
<i>and so he spoke ♪</i>

685
00:51:55,753 --> 00:52:01,892
<i>♪ That Lord of Castamere ♪</i>

686
00:52:01,926 --> 00:52:04,828
<i>♪ And now the rains ♪</i>

687
00:52:04,863 --> 00:52:07,998
<i>♪ Weep o'er his hall ♪</i>

688
00:52:08,032 --> 00:52:14,438
<i>♪ With no one there to hear ♪</i>

689
00:52:14,472 --> 00:52:17,074
<i>♪ Yes, now the rains ♪</i>

690
00:52:17,108 --> 00:52:20,544
<i>♪ Weep o'er his hall ♪</i>

691
00:52:20,578 --> 00:52:26,416
<i>♪ And not a soul</i>
<i>to hear. ♪</i>



## S04E06

﻿1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:04,162
WWW.MY-SUBS.COM

1
00:00:05,941 --> 00:00:09,093
<i>(theme music playing)</i>

2
00:01:38,206 --> 00:01:48,916
<font color="#ec14bd">Sync & corrections by honeybunny</font>
<font color="#ec14bd">www.MY-SUBS.com</font>

3
00:01:53,449 --> 00:01:55,700
<i>(seagulls calling)</i>

4
00:01:59,455 --> 00:02:02,090
(men shouting)

5
00:02:46,969 --> 00:02:49,670
Your Grace, if you'd like
to sit, I'm sure that--

6
00:02:49,722 --> 00:02:51,973
We've been here since midday.

7
00:03:00,766 --> 00:03:04,102
Easterners have a difference
sense of time, I've often found.

8
00:03:04,104 --> 00:03:08,106
Once I was waiting for Salladhor
Saan here in Braavos.

9
00:03:09,525 --> 00:03:10,775
Together, we were going to run

10
00:03:10,826 --> 00:03:13,327
three shiploads
of the finest--

11
00:03:31,296 --> 00:03:34,382
<i>(doors close)</i>

12
00:03:40,556 --> 00:03:42,740
Welcome to the Iron Bank.

13
00:03:42,775 --> 00:03:46,027
Please, sit.

14
00:04:08,717 --> 00:04:11,135
What can we do
for you, Lord Stannis?

15
00:04:11,170 --> 00:04:13,137
This is Stannis
of the House Baratheon,

16
00:04:13,172 --> 00:04:15,406
<i>King of the Andals</i>
<i>and the First Men,</i>

17
00:04:15,440 --> 00:04:18,376
Lord of the Seven Kingdoms
and Protector of the Realm.

18
00:04:22,231 --> 00:04:24,565
<i>The Iron Throne</i>
<i>is currently occupied</i>

19
00:04:24,600 --> 00:04:26,484
by Tommen
of the House Baratheon,

20
00:04:26,518 --> 00:04:28,019
King of the Andals
and the First Men,

21
00:04:28,021 --> 00:04:30,938
Lord of the Seven Kingdoms
and Protector of the Realm.

22
00:04:30,989 --> 00:04:33,658
He shares no blood with me.

23
00:04:33,692 --> 00:04:37,078
He is a bastard born of incest.

24
00:04:37,112 --> 00:04:39,163
<i>As was his brother before him.</i>

25
00:04:39,198 --> 00:04:41,949
Yes, we have heard this story.

26
00:04:42,000 --> 00:04:44,619
It's not a story.
It's the truth.

27
00:04:44,670 --> 00:04:48,005
The king's grandfather
tells a different story.

28
00:04:48,040 --> 00:04:50,424
A story about a jealous uncle

29
00:04:50,459 --> 00:04:52,660
whose attempts to usurp the
throne from the rightful king

30
00:04:52,694 --> 00:04:57,632
cost the Seven Kingdoms
dearly in blood and gold.

31
00:04:57,683 --> 00:04:59,383
Gold you loaned him.

32
00:04:59,385 --> 00:05:03,621
And you feel your blood gives
you a claim on our gold?

33
00:05:03,655 --> 00:05:06,274
More than any man living.

34
00:05:06,308 --> 00:05:09,227
Across the Narrow Sea,
your books are filled

35
00:05:09,261 --> 00:05:12,396
with words like
"usurper" and "madman"

36
00:05:12,431 --> 00:05:14,932
and "blood right."

37
00:05:14,967 --> 00:05:18,102
Here, our books
are filled with numbers.

38
00:05:18,136 --> 00:05:20,321
We prefer the stories they tell.

39
00:05:20,355 --> 00:05:22,273
More plain.

40
00:05:22,307 --> 00:05:25,743
Less open to interpretation.

41
00:05:27,913 --> 00:05:30,865
How many fighting men
remain loyal to you?

42
00:05:30,899 --> 00:05:32,250
4,000.

43
00:05:32,301 --> 00:05:34,252
<i>And how many ships do you have?</i>

44
00:05:34,303 --> 00:05:36,554
<i>The ones still afloat,</i>
<i>Ser Davos,</i>

45
00:05:36,588 --> 00:05:39,056
not at the bottom
of Blackwater Bay.

46
00:05:40,676 --> 00:05:42,426
32.

47
00:05:42,477 --> 00:05:44,228
And how much wheat
and barley and beef

48
00:05:44,263 --> 00:05:46,597
and pork do you produce
on Dragonstone

49
00:05:46,599 --> 00:05:51,385
to feed your 4,000 men
on your 32 ships?

50
00:05:53,572 --> 00:05:55,406
None.

51
00:05:55,440 --> 00:05:58,276
You can see why these numbers
seem unlikely to add up

52
00:05:58,278 --> 00:06:01,445
to a happy ending
from our perspective.

53
00:06:02,848 --> 00:06:05,216
<i>I'm afraid we must respectfully</i>

54
00:06:05,250 --> 00:06:08,302
<i>decline your request.</i>

55
00:06:08,337 --> 00:06:11,455
<i>But we thank you for paying</i>
<i>us the honor of your visit.</i>

56
00:06:20,432 --> 00:06:21,632
My lord.

57
00:06:21,683 --> 00:06:24,385
I'm not a lord,
Ser Davos Seaworth.

58
00:06:24,436 --> 00:06:26,554
You would not be either here.

59
00:06:26,588 --> 00:06:28,506
In Braavos,

60
00:06:28,540 --> 00:06:31,776
thieves are not
rewarded with titles.

61
00:06:31,810 --> 00:06:33,311
Well, strictly speaking,
I didn't do the thieving.

62
00:06:33,362 --> 00:06:34,946
That would be the pirates.

63
00:06:34,980 --> 00:06:38,366
I just moved what they stole
from one place to another.

64
00:06:39,868 --> 00:06:41,902
This is the payment
that was demanded

65
00:06:41,954 --> 00:06:43,237
by King Stannis for my crimes.

66
00:06:43,288 --> 00:06:45,656
I consider it
an honest accounting.

67
00:06:45,707 --> 00:06:48,159
He's an honest man
and he's your best chance

68
00:06:48,210 --> 00:06:50,661
to get back the money
you've sunk into Westeros.

69
00:06:50,663 --> 00:06:53,464
Which is a lot, I imagine.
Wars are expensive.

70
00:06:53,565 --> 00:06:54,832
The war is over.

71
00:06:54,834 --> 00:06:57,335
As long as Stannis lives,
the war is not over.

72
00:06:57,337 --> 00:06:59,337
Who's the real power
in King's Landing?

73
00:06:59,339 --> 00:07:01,505
- Ser Davos...
- Humor me.

74
00:07:01,507 --> 00:07:05,142
- <i> Tywin Lannister.</i>
- How old is Tywin Lannister?

75
00:07:05,177 --> 00:07:08,579
- 67.
- 67. And when he dies, who's in command?

76
00:07:08,613 --> 00:07:10,681
A half-grown boy,

77
00:07:10,732 --> 00:07:13,017
<i>the product of incest?</i>

78
00:07:13,051 --> 00:07:15,853
Cersei Lannister, a queen
whose people despise her?

79
00:07:15,855 --> 00:07:18,556
Jaime Lannister, a man best
known for killing the king

80
00:07:18,590 --> 00:07:20,691
<i>he was sworn to protect?</i>

81
00:07:20,726 --> 00:07:24,362
When Tywin's gone,
who do you back?

82
00:07:24,413 --> 00:07:27,031
That is a problem
for another time.

83
00:07:27,082 --> 00:07:29,333
Begging your pardon,
I think it's a problem for now.

84
00:07:30,702 --> 00:07:33,504
There's only one reliable
leader left in Westeros.

85
00:07:33,538 --> 00:07:35,339
Stannis.

86
00:07:35,374 --> 00:07:37,208
He's got the birthright.

87
00:07:37,259 --> 00:07:39,377
He's in his prime.

88
00:07:39,428 --> 00:07:41,128
He's a tried and tested
battle commander.

89
00:07:41,179 --> 00:07:43,381
And he doesn't just talk
about paying people back,

90
00:07:43,432 --> 00:07:44,765
he does it.

91
00:07:48,637 --> 00:07:51,889
Saan:<i> The lookout sees a
pirate ship sailing their way.</i>

92
00:07:51,891 --> 00:07:53,891
<i>The captain shouts</i>
<i>to his first mate,</i>

93
00:07:53,942 --> 00:07:55,776
"Bring me
my red shirt."

94
00:07:55,811 --> 00:07:58,779
The first mate brings the red
shirt and the captain puts it on.

95
00:07:58,814 --> 00:08:00,731
And when the pirates
try to board,

96
00:08:00,733 --> 00:08:03,117
the brave captain leads
his men to victory.

97
00:08:03,151 --> 00:08:06,537
A few days later,
the lookout screams,

98
00:08:06,571 --> 00:08:08,372
"Two pirate ships!"

99
00:08:08,407 --> 00:08:12,209
The crew is shivering
like scared mice.

100
00:08:12,244 --> 00:08:14,211
But the courageous
captain hollers,

101
00:08:14,246 --> 00:08:16,047
"Bring me
my red shirt!"

102
00:08:16,081 --> 00:08:18,299
<i>After the battle,</i>
<i>the first mate asks,</i>

103
00:08:18,333 --> 00:08:22,169
"Captain, why do you call for
your red shirt before battle?"

104
00:08:22,220 --> 00:08:23,754
The captain replies,

105
00:08:23,805 --> 00:08:27,591
"So that if I am stabbed,
you will not see me bleed."

106
00:08:27,642 --> 00:08:29,844
The next morning
the lookout screams,

107
00:08:29,895 --> 00:08:32,346
"10 pirate ships!

108
00:08:32,397 --> 00:08:35,182
- We are surrounded!"
- (women laugh)

109
00:08:35,267 --> 00:08:36,984
<i>The crew goes silent.</i>

110
00:08:37,018 --> 00:08:39,520
<i>They all look</i>
<i>to their brave captain,</i>

111
00:08:39,571 --> 00:08:41,655
<i>waiting for his usual command.</i>

112
00:08:41,690 --> 00:08:43,691
<i>Calm as ever,</i>
<i>the captain bellows...</i>

113
00:08:43,742 --> 00:08:46,110
Women:
"Bring me my brown pants!"

114
00:08:46,161 --> 00:08:48,913
(laughs)

115
00:08:48,947 --> 00:08:52,416
You think they ever met a pirate
who didn't tell them that joke?

116
00:08:52,451 --> 00:08:55,870
- Davos.
- (chuckles)

117
00:08:55,954 --> 00:08:58,289
I heard you were rotting in
a dungeon in Dragonstone.

118
00:08:58,291 --> 00:09:00,624
Only half rotted.

119
00:09:00,675 --> 00:09:03,177
(laughs)

120
00:09:03,211 --> 00:09:05,346
Join us, my friend.
Join us.

121
00:09:05,380 --> 00:09:08,299
- This is Lhara.
- (gasps)

122
00:09:08,301 --> 00:09:09,900
- And this is--
- I am Lhara.

123
00:09:09,935 --> 00:09:11,635
(squeals)

124
00:09:11,637 --> 00:09:13,137
(laughs)

125
00:09:13,139 --> 00:09:15,606
She's an artist,
this one, truly.

126
00:09:15,640 --> 00:09:18,976
No time, I'm afraid.
We sail at sunrise.

127
00:09:19,027 --> 00:09:20,444
We?

128
00:09:20,479 --> 00:09:22,646
You, me-- we.

129
00:09:22,697 --> 00:09:24,782
(scoffs)

130
00:09:24,816 --> 00:09:27,985
Once I thought
this man loved me.

131
00:09:27,987 --> 00:09:32,156
<i>Now I know he despises me.</i>

132
00:09:32,158 --> 00:09:36,544
He wants to see me die poor
and alone on some cold--

133
00:09:46,505 --> 00:09:48,172
You won't be alone.

134
00:09:48,174 --> 00:09:49,723
And you won't be poor.

135
00:09:57,516 --> 00:10:00,100
There's a chest of the good
stuff left back at your house.

136
00:10:00,152 --> 00:10:02,019
I gave it to your wife.

137
00:10:02,021 --> 00:10:05,272
(women laugh)

138
00:10:05,323 --> 00:10:07,691
You're not my friend, my friend.

139
00:10:07,742 --> 00:10:09,443
(laughs)

140
00:10:09,494 --> 00:10:11,328
I'll see you at sunrise.

141
00:10:14,332 --> 00:10:16,584
Yara: "I give you
until the full moon

142
00:10:16,618 --> 00:10:20,204
to order all ironborn
scum out of the North

143
00:10:20,255 --> 00:10:23,924
<i>and back to those shit-stained
rocks you call home.</i>

144
00:10:23,959 --> 00:10:25,960
On the first night
of the full moon,

145
00:10:26,011 --> 00:10:28,762
I will hunt down every
islander still on our lands

146
00:10:28,797 --> 00:10:30,714
and flay them living,

147
00:10:30,716 --> 00:10:33,384
the way I flayed
the 20 ironborn scum

148
00:10:33,435 --> 00:10:35,019
I found at Winterfell.

149
00:10:36,271 --> 00:10:38,889
<i>In the box you'll find</i>
<i>a special gift--</i>

150
00:10:39,941 --> 00:10:42,393
<i>Theon's favorite toy.</i>

151
00:10:42,444 --> 00:10:45,229
<i>He cried when I took</i>
<i>it away from him.</i>

152
00:10:46,615 --> 00:10:49,400
<i>Leave the North now</i>
<i>or more boxes will follow</i>

153
00:10:49,402 --> 00:10:51,569
<i>with more Theon.</i>

154
00:10:51,620 --> 00:10:53,621
<i>Signed Ramsay Snow,</i>

155
00:10:53,655 --> 00:10:57,074
<i>natural born son</i>
<i>of Roose Bolton,</i>

156
00:10:57,076 --> 00:10:59,076
<i>Lord of the Dreadfort</i>

157
00:10:59,078 --> 00:11:02,046
<i>and Warden of the North."</i>

158
00:11:02,080 --> 00:11:04,048
Yes!

159
00:11:04,132 --> 00:11:06,217
Yara:
<i>They skinned our countrymen</i>

160
00:11:06,251 --> 00:11:09,003
<i>and they mutilated my brother,</i>

161
00:11:09,054 --> 00:11:11,388
<i>your prince.</i>

162
00:11:11,423 --> 00:11:13,474
<i>Your prince.</i>

163
00:11:13,508 --> 00:11:16,260
<i>Everything they've done to him,</i>

164
00:11:16,262 --> 00:11:18,345
<i>they've also done to you.</i>

165
00:11:18,396 --> 00:11:19,813
Yes! Yes!

166
00:11:19,848 --> 00:11:23,767
Yara: <i>As long as they can hurt
our prince with impunity,</i>

167
00:11:23,769 --> 00:11:27,771
<i>the word ironborn means nothing.</i>

168
00:11:29,024 --> 00:11:31,942
- (moaning loudly)
- <i> (metal clinking)</i>

169
00:11:33,912 --> 00:11:37,114
<i>(Myranda moaning)</i>

170
00:11:40,252 --> 00:11:42,503
(grunts)

171
00:11:51,713 --> 00:11:53,631
Go left.

172
00:11:53,682 --> 00:11:56,133
(men whispering)

173
00:12:00,388 --> 00:12:01,972
(groans)

174
00:12:01,974 --> 00:12:04,808
- Theon Greyjoy.
- I don't know--

175
00:12:04,893 --> 00:12:06,193
I'm here for Theon Greyjoy.

176
00:12:06,227 --> 00:12:07,561
Take me to the dungeons.

177
00:12:07,612 --> 00:12:09,813
He's not in the dungeons.

178
00:12:12,651 --> 00:12:14,818
(dogs panting)

179
00:12:21,076 --> 00:12:23,911
(raspy breathing)

180
00:12:28,700 --> 00:12:30,334
<i>(door opens)</i>

181
00:12:32,304 --> 00:12:34,138
- <i> (alarm ringing)</i>
- <i> (men shouting)</i>

182
00:12:34,172 --> 00:12:36,340
- Last cage on the right.
- Thank you.

183
00:12:36,391 --> 00:12:39,009
(gurgles)

184
00:12:39,060 --> 00:12:40,844
- <i> (swords clanging)</i>
- Man:<i> This way!</i>

185
00:12:40,895 --> 00:12:42,813
(barking)

186
00:12:48,403 --> 00:12:50,270
(whimpering)

187
00:12:54,693 --> 00:12:56,243
We're going home.

188
00:12:58,079 --> 00:12:59,997
No!

189
00:13:02,200 --> 00:13:06,170
It's all right.
It's me, Yara.

190
00:13:06,204 --> 00:13:08,339
You can't trick me.

191
00:13:08,373 --> 00:13:10,541
Tell him. Tell him you
couldn't trick me.

192
00:13:10,543 --> 00:13:12,376
I'm not tricking you, Theon.
I'm saving you.

193
00:13:12,378 --> 00:13:13,761
Not Theon! Reek!

194
00:13:13,845 --> 00:13:15,546
- Reek! My name is Reek!
- First mate:<i> They're here.</i>

195
00:13:15,548 --> 00:13:17,047
If they catch us
in here, we're trapped.

196
00:13:17,049 --> 00:13:18,716
Help me with him.

197
00:13:18,767 --> 00:13:20,634
No, you can't!

198
00:13:20,685 --> 00:13:22,436
- You can't!
- You're Theon Greyjoy.

199
00:13:22,470 --> 00:13:26,607
No, I don't believe her!
I know who I am!

200
00:13:26,641 --> 00:13:29,893
I'm Reek! Reek!
Loyal Reek!

201
00:13:29,944 --> 00:13:32,730
Good Reek!
I've always been Reek!

202
00:13:34,115 --> 00:13:36,817
- <i> (dogs barking)</i>
- <i> (Theon whimpering)</i>

203
00:13:43,124 --> 00:13:46,076
This is turning into
a lovely evening.

204
00:13:46,995 --> 00:13:49,463
(shouting)

205
00:13:58,256 --> 00:13:59,923
(snarling, barking)

206
00:13:59,974 --> 00:14:02,426
- (groans)
- (laughs)

207
00:14:02,477 --> 00:14:03,977
Theon:
<i>I'm Reek!</i>

208
00:14:04,012 --> 00:14:05,929
Loyal Reek!

209
00:14:13,154 --> 00:14:15,355
Aah!
Theon!

210
00:14:28,638 --> 00:14:34,840
- Give me my brother!
- You've got bigger balls than he ever did.

211
00:14:36,726 --> 00:14:40,595
But with those big balls
of yours...

212
00:14:45,601 --> 00:14:47,769
how fast can you run?

213
00:14:49,656 --> 00:14:52,824
<i>(cage rattling)</i>

214
00:14:55,278 --> 00:14:57,446
<i>(dogs barking)</i>

215
00:14:59,332 --> 00:15:01,249
Make for the ship, now.

216
00:15:01,284 --> 00:15:02,951
But your brother.

217
00:15:03,002 --> 00:15:04,953
My brother is dead.

218
00:15:12,545 --> 00:15:15,430
Ramsay:
I have a treat for you.

219
00:15:15,465 --> 00:15:17,132
A reward.

220
00:15:17,183 --> 00:15:19,718
Reward?

221
00:15:20,853 --> 00:15:22,020
Yes, Reek.

222
00:15:22,055 --> 00:15:24,973
Those creatures
who came in the night,

223
00:15:24,975 --> 00:15:27,642
they wanted to take you away.

224
00:15:27,644 --> 00:15:29,611
And you didn't let them.

225
00:15:29,645 --> 00:15:32,047
You remained loyal.

226
00:15:32,081 --> 00:15:35,984
I-- I didn't want
them to take me.

227
00:15:37,320 --> 00:15:38,737
I was so scared.

228
00:15:38,788 --> 00:15:41,073
- I didn't want them--
- Yes, yes, Reek.

229
00:15:50,083 --> 00:15:52,300
It's a bath.

230
00:15:52,335 --> 00:15:54,669
For you.

231
00:15:57,840 --> 00:16:00,392
Remove those rags.

232
00:16:01,594 --> 00:16:03,428
Now.

233
00:16:20,079 --> 00:16:22,030
The britches, too, Reek.

234
00:16:22,081 --> 00:16:23,698
Take them off.

235
00:16:47,390 --> 00:16:49,858
(whimpering)

236
00:16:58,468 --> 00:17:01,286
(water splashes)

237
00:17:24,977 --> 00:17:26,645
(whimpers)

238
00:17:42,795 --> 00:17:44,946
(gasps)

239
00:17:49,952 --> 00:17:52,621
Do you love me, Reek?

240
00:17:58,511 --> 00:18:01,129
Yes, of course, my lord.

241
00:18:02,882 --> 00:18:04,382
Good.

242
00:18:04,433 --> 00:18:06,768
Because I need you
to do something for me.

243
00:18:06,802 --> 00:18:08,854
Something very important.

244
00:18:08,888 --> 00:18:12,140
There's a castle, you see.

245
00:18:12,191 --> 00:18:15,277
Some bad men hold this castle.

246
00:18:16,646 --> 00:18:19,481
I need your help
to take this castle back.

247
00:18:23,035 --> 00:18:24,786
But how can--

248
00:18:27,156 --> 00:18:29,324
I need you to play a role.

249
00:18:30,493 --> 00:18:33,411
To pretend to be someone
you're not.

250
00:18:36,999 --> 00:18:39,167
Pretend to be who?

251
00:18:39,169 --> 00:18:42,003
Theon Greyjoy.

252
00:18:45,508 --> 00:18:47,842
(goats bleating)

253
00:18:57,253 --> 00:18:59,237
<i>(water splashes)</i>

254
00:19:04,143 --> 00:19:05,994
<i>(water splashes)</i>

255
00:19:12,418 --> 00:19:14,703
<i>(rock thuds)</i>

256
00:19:23,296 --> 00:19:25,213
(roaring)

257
00:19:32,888 --> 00:19:35,523
(bleating)

258
00:19:38,694 --> 00:19:41,396
(roaring)

259
00:19:50,573 --> 00:19:54,859
_

260
00:19:55,233 --> 00:19:56,755
_

261
00:19:56,875 --> 00:19:57,753
_

262
00:19:57,873 --> 00:19:59,524
_

263
00:19:59,644 --> 00:20:01,994
_

264
00:20:02,254 --> 00:20:04,399
_

265
00:20:04,519 --> 00:20:08,126
_

266
00:20:08,742 --> 00:20:11,174
_

267
00:20:13,413 --> 00:20:15,546
_

268
00:20:24,273 --> 00:20:26,170
(speaking Low Valyrian)

269
00:20:28,494 --> 00:20:29,894
He is a goatherd.

270
00:20:29,929 --> 00:20:33,465
He says he prayed for your victory
against the slave masters.

271
00:20:33,499 --> 00:20:36,101
I thank him for his prayers.

272
00:20:36,135 --> 00:20:38,203
(speaks Low Valyrian)

273
00:20:45,678 --> 00:20:48,496
(speaking Low Valyrian)

274
00:20:51,634 --> 00:20:53,601
It was your dragons, he says.

275
00:20:53,636 --> 00:20:57,188
<i>They came this morning</i>
<i>for his flock.</i>

276
00:20:57,223 --> 00:21:01,209
(speaks Low Valyrian)

277
00:21:01,243 --> 00:21:03,311
He hopes he has not
offended Your Grace,

278
00:21:03,346 --> 00:21:04,979
but now he has nothing.

279
00:21:06,399 --> 00:21:08,199
Tell this man I am sorry
for his hardship.

280
00:21:08,234 --> 00:21:12,053
- (translating)
- <i> I cannot bring back his goats,</i>

281
00:21:12,088 --> 00:21:16,908
but I will see he is paid
their value three times over.

282
00:21:18,994 --> 00:21:21,329
(speaking Low Valyrian)

283
00:21:33,309 --> 00:21:35,510
Send the next one in.

284
00:21:43,363 --> 00:21:48,627
_

285
00:21:49,693 --> 00:21:56,127
_

286
00:22:09,512 --> 00:22:11,179
Queen Daenerys.

287
00:22:11,213 --> 00:22:13,264
Tales of your beauty
were not exaggerated.

288
00:22:13,299 --> 00:22:15,350
I thank you.

289
00:22:15,384 --> 00:22:18,386
Mine is one of the oldest and
proudest families in Meereen.

290
00:22:18,421 --> 00:22:21,055
Then it is my honor
to receive you.

291
00:22:21,090 --> 00:22:24,025
My father, one of
Meereen's most respected

292
00:22:24,059 --> 00:22:27,028
and beloved citizens,
oversaw the restoration

293
00:22:27,062 --> 00:22:29,364
and maintenance
of its greatest landmarks.

294
00:22:29,398 --> 00:22:30,915
This pyramid included.

295
00:22:30,950 --> 00:22:33,118
For that, he has my gratitude.

296
00:22:33,152 --> 00:22:34,819
I should be honored to meet him.

297
00:22:34,854 --> 00:22:36,304
You have, Your Grace.

298
00:22:36,338 --> 00:22:38,139
You crucified him.

299
00:22:40,376 --> 00:22:42,410
I pray you'll never live to see

300
00:22:42,461 --> 00:22:44,412
a member of your family
treated so cruelly.

301
00:22:44,463 --> 00:22:47,982
Your father crucified
innocent children.

302
00:22:48,050 --> 00:22:51,469
My father spoke out against
crucifying those children.

303
00:22:51,504 --> 00:22:54,706
He decried it as a criminal
act, but was overruled.

304
00:22:54,740 --> 00:22:58,076
Is it justice to answer
one crime with another?

305
00:22:58,144 --> 00:23:00,562
I am sorry you no longer
have a father,

306
00:23:00,596 --> 00:23:03,932
but my treatment
of the masters was no crime.

307
00:23:03,983 --> 00:23:06,751
You'd be wise to remember that.

308
00:23:06,785 --> 00:23:09,737
What's done is done.

309
00:23:09,772 --> 00:23:12,907
You are the queen and I
am a servant of Meereen.

310
00:23:12,942 --> 00:23:16,611
A servant who does not wish to
see its traditions eradicated.

311
00:23:16,613 --> 00:23:19,447
And what traditions
do you speak of?

312
00:23:19,449 --> 00:23:21,449
<i>The tradition of funeral rite.</i>

313
00:23:21,500 --> 00:23:23,785
Proper burial in
the Temple of the Graces.

314
00:23:23,836 --> 00:23:28,373
My father and 162
noble Meereenese

315
00:23:28,424 --> 00:23:30,758
are still nailed to those posts,

316
00:23:30,793 --> 00:23:33,962
carrion for vultures,
rotting in the sun.

317
00:23:43,172 --> 00:23:46,024
Your Grace, I ask that you
order these men taken down

318
00:23:46,058 --> 00:23:48,276
so that they might receive
proper burials.

319
00:23:48,310 --> 00:23:52,480
And what of the slave children
these noble Meereenese crucified?

320
00:23:52,482 --> 00:23:54,983
They were rotting
in the sun as well.

321
00:23:54,985 --> 00:23:58,653
<i>Would you have begged me for
their right to a proper burial?</i>

322
00:23:58,704 --> 00:24:01,406
Your Grace, I cannot defend
the actions of the masters.

323
00:24:01,490 --> 00:24:05,376
I can only speak to you as a
son who loved his father.

324
00:24:05,411 --> 00:24:07,662
<i>Let me take his body down.</i>

325
00:24:07,664 --> 00:24:10,548
<i>Let me have him brought</i>
<i>to the temple</i>

326
00:24:10,583 --> 00:24:12,083
and buried with dignity

327
00:24:12,134 --> 00:24:14,502
so that he might find
peace in the next world.

328
00:24:17,740 --> 00:24:21,392
Bury your father,
Hizdahr zo Loraq.

329
00:24:25,180 --> 00:24:28,399
Thank you, my queen.

330
00:24:39,945 --> 00:24:41,379
How many more?

331
00:24:41,413 --> 00:24:44,699
There are 212 supplicants
waiting, Your Grace.

332
00:24:44,783 --> 00:24:47,535
212?

333
00:24:55,010 --> 00:24:57,512
Send the next one in.

334
00:24:59,431 --> 00:25:02,133
These meetings aren't always going
to be this early, are they?

335
00:25:02,184 --> 00:25:05,270
I was up late last night.

336
00:25:05,304 --> 00:25:09,307
So, does this mean I am a
master of something now?

337
00:25:09,358 --> 00:25:11,225
Coins, ships?

338
00:25:11,227 --> 00:25:13,478
Lord Tywin and I
already determined

339
00:25:13,529 --> 00:25:16,731
that I shall be the Master
of Ships long before you--

340
00:25:21,570 --> 00:25:23,738
Lord Tywin, it's a great honor

341
00:25:23,789 --> 00:25:26,240
to have been granted a
seat on this council. I--

342
00:25:26,242 --> 00:25:28,242
Tywin: <i>The trial
begins this afternoon.</i>

343
00:25:28,294 --> 00:25:30,461
We only have the morning
for affairs of state.

344
00:25:30,496 --> 00:25:31,963
Shall we begin?

345
00:25:31,997 --> 00:25:33,998
Varys: Sandor Clegane
has been spotted

346
00:25:34,049 --> 00:25:36,834
in the Riverlands, my lord.

347
00:25:36,885 --> 00:25:38,219
A coward and a traitor.

348
00:25:38,253 --> 00:25:42,507
My birds tell me the Hound
slaughtered five of our soldiers.

349
00:25:42,558 --> 00:25:45,259
I believe the phrase
"fuck the king" was uttered.

350
00:25:45,261 --> 00:25:47,061
Disgraceful.

351
00:25:47,096 --> 00:25:48,930
Tywin: <i>What would it take
to make the common soldier</i>

352
00:25:48,932 --> 00:25:51,232
stupid enough to try
his luck with the Hound?

353
00:25:51,266 --> 00:25:53,835
10 silver stags
seems a generous bounty.

354
00:25:53,869 --> 00:25:56,688
Make it 100.
What else?

355
00:25:56,739 --> 00:25:59,273
More whispers
from the east, my lord.

356
00:25:59,325 --> 00:26:00,858
The Targaryen girl?

357
00:26:00,909 --> 00:26:03,695
Daenerys has taken up
residence in Meereen.

358
00:26:03,746 --> 00:26:07,348
She has conquered the city
and rules as its queen.

359
00:26:07,383 --> 00:26:08,950
Conquered with what?

360
00:26:09,001 --> 00:26:10,918
Varys: <i>She commands an army
of Unsullied, my queen.</i>

361
00:26:10,953 --> 00:26:12,954
Some 8,000 strong.

362
00:26:12,988 --> 00:26:15,289
She has a company
of sellswords--

363
00:26:15,291 --> 00:26:16,674
the Second Sons.

364
00:26:16,709 --> 00:26:19,127
<i>She has two knights</i>
<i>advising her--</i>

365
00:26:19,161 --> 00:26:22,430
Jorah Mormont
and Barristan Selmy.

366
00:26:22,464 --> 00:26:24,465
And she has three dragons.

367
00:26:24,516 --> 00:26:25,933
Baby dragons.

368
00:26:25,968 --> 00:26:28,102
Larger every year, Your Grace.

369
00:26:28,137 --> 00:26:31,606
Mormont is spying on her for us.

370
00:26:31,640 --> 00:26:34,609
No longer. He appears
to be fully devoted to her.

371
00:26:34,643 --> 00:26:37,645
As for Ser Barristan,
it would seem

372
00:26:37,680 --> 00:26:40,314
he took his dismissal
from the Kingsguard

373
00:26:40,316 --> 00:26:42,150
a bit harder than anticipated.

374
00:26:42,152 --> 00:26:44,068
He's an old man.

375
00:26:44,119 --> 00:26:46,020
He wasn't fit to protect my son.

376
00:26:46,055 --> 00:26:48,072
Joffrey didn't die on his watch.

377
00:26:48,123 --> 00:26:51,409
Dismissing him was as
insulting as it was stupid.

378
00:26:51,460 --> 00:26:54,562
Don't tell me you're worried about
a child halfway across the world.

379
00:26:54,596 --> 00:26:58,166
A child with two seasoned
warriors counseling her

380
00:26:58,200 --> 00:27:01,419
and a powerful army
at her back, Your Grace.

381
00:27:01,470 --> 00:27:03,504
Lord Varys is right.

382
00:27:03,506 --> 00:27:06,007
I have been to Essos and seen
the Unsullied firsthand.

383
00:27:06,058 --> 00:27:09,577
They are very impressive
on the battlefield.

384
00:27:09,611 --> 00:27:11,345
Less so in the bedroom.

385
00:27:11,347 --> 00:27:14,182
Dragons haven't won
a war in 300 years.

386
00:27:14,184 --> 00:27:16,734
Armies win them all the time.

387
00:27:16,769 --> 00:27:19,020
She must be dealt with.

388
00:27:19,054 --> 00:27:21,072
How, my lord?

389
00:27:21,106 --> 00:27:22,440
By force?

390
00:27:22,491 --> 00:27:24,509
Eventually, if it comes to that.

391
00:27:26,662 --> 00:27:29,614
Can your little birds
find their way into Meereen?

392
00:27:29,648 --> 00:27:31,666
Most certainly, my Lord Hand.

393
00:27:31,700 --> 00:27:34,619
Mmm. Lord Tyrell,
be a good man.

394
00:27:34,670 --> 00:27:36,370
<i>Fetch my quill and paper.</i>

395
00:27:48,133 --> 00:27:51,219
<i>(footsteps)</i>

396
00:27:57,276 --> 00:27:59,444
Prince Oberyn.

397
00:27:59,478 --> 00:28:01,896
Lord Varys...

398
00:28:01,898 --> 00:28:05,133
Only Varys.
I'm not actually a nobleman.

399
00:28:05,167 --> 00:28:07,702
No one is under obligation
to call me lord.

400
00:28:07,736 --> 00:28:09,570
And yet everyone does.

401
00:28:12,074 --> 00:28:14,542
You seem quite knowledgeable
about the Unsullied.

402
00:28:14,576 --> 00:28:16,494
Did you spend much time
in Essos?

403
00:28:16,578 --> 00:28:19,213
Five years.

404
00:28:19,248 --> 00:28:20,832
May I ask why?

405
00:28:20,883 --> 00:28:23,718
'Tis a big and beautiful world.

406
00:28:23,752 --> 00:28:26,420
Most of us live and die

407
00:28:26,422 --> 00:28:28,139
in the same corner
where we were born

408
00:28:28,173 --> 00:28:31,092
and never get to see any of it.

409
00:28:31,094 --> 00:28:33,728
I don't want to be most of us.

410
00:28:33,762 --> 00:28:35,763
Most of us aren't princes.

411
00:28:37,132 --> 00:28:40,601
(chuckles)
You are from Essos.

412
00:28:41,820 --> 00:28:43,488
Where? Lys?

413
00:28:44,807 --> 00:28:46,524
<i>I have an ear for accents.</i>

414
00:28:46,575 --> 00:28:48,242
I've lost my accent entirely.

415
00:28:48,277 --> 00:28:49,777
I have an ear for that as well.

416
00:28:51,663 --> 00:28:53,831
<i>How did you get here?</i>

417
00:28:53,866 --> 00:28:55,449
It's a long story.

418
00:28:55,534 --> 00:28:58,452
One you don't like
telling people.

419
00:28:58,504 --> 00:29:01,122
People I trust.

420
00:29:01,124 --> 00:29:04,208
My paramour Ellaria, she would
find you very interesting.

421
00:29:04,259 --> 00:29:06,544
You should come to
the brothel and meet her.

422
00:29:06,595 --> 00:29:09,630
We brought our own wine, not
the swill they serve here.

423
00:29:09,632 --> 00:29:12,800
We have some lovely
boys on retainer, but...

424
00:29:15,704 --> 00:29:18,105
You did like boys before?

425
00:29:20,192 --> 00:29:22,059
Really?

426
00:29:22,110 --> 00:29:23,978
Girls? Hmm.

427
00:29:23,980 --> 00:29:26,647
I hope you won't be offended when
I say I never would have guessed.

428
00:29:26,698 --> 00:29:28,149
Not at all.

429
00:29:28,151 --> 00:29:30,985
But I was never interested
in girls, either.

430
00:29:31,036 --> 00:29:33,454
- What then?
- <i> Nothing.</i>

431
00:29:33,488 --> 00:29:36,290
Everybody is interested
in something.

432
00:29:36,325 --> 00:29:38,075
<i>Not me.</i>

433
00:29:38,126 --> 00:29:40,328
When I see what
desire does to people,

434
00:29:40,330 --> 00:29:42,296
what it's done to this country,

435
00:29:42,331 --> 00:29:45,166
I am very glad
to have no part in it.

436
00:29:46,301 --> 00:29:49,637
<i>Besides, the absence of desire</i>

437
00:29:49,671 --> 00:29:52,757
leaves one free to pursue
other things.

438
00:29:52,808 --> 00:29:54,475
Such as?

439
00:30:19,334 --> 00:30:21,586
<i>(keys clinking)</i>

440
00:30:29,595 --> 00:30:32,680
Let me guess,
I've been pardoned.

441
00:30:48,897 --> 00:30:50,731
Really?

442
00:30:52,234 --> 00:30:53,734
Father's orders.

443
00:30:53,785 --> 00:30:57,571
Well, we mustn't
disappoint Father.

444
00:31:03,278 --> 00:31:05,963
(crowd murmuring)

445
00:31:07,950 --> 00:31:09,917
Man:
<i>Kingslayer!</i>

446
00:31:55,881 --> 00:31:59,050
I, Tommen
of the House Baratheon,

447
00:31:59,101 --> 00:32:00,801
<i>First of my Name,</i>

448
00:32:00,852 --> 00:32:03,137
King of the Andals
and the First Men,

449
00:32:03,188 --> 00:32:05,806
and Lord of the Seven Kingdoms,

450
00:32:05,808 --> 00:32:09,560
do hereby recuse myself
from this trial.

451
00:32:09,611 --> 00:32:12,730
Tywin of the House Lannister,
Hand of the King,

452
00:32:12,781 --> 00:32:16,667
Protector of the Realm,
will sit as judge in my stead.

453
00:32:17,986 --> 00:32:20,621
And with him Prince Oberyn
of the House Martell

454
00:32:20,655 --> 00:32:23,941
<i>and Lord Mace</i>
<i>of the House Tyrell.</i>

455
00:32:23,975 --> 00:32:26,327
And if found guilty...

456
00:32:28,497 --> 00:32:30,247
may the gods punish the accused.

457
00:33:05,867 --> 00:33:08,252
Tyrion of the House Lannister,

458
00:33:08,286 --> 00:33:12,373
you stand accused by
the Queen Regent of regicide.

459
00:33:13,675 --> 00:33:16,043
Did you kill King Joffrey?

460
00:33:17,379 --> 00:33:18,679
No.

461
00:33:18,713 --> 00:33:21,048
Did your wife, the Lady Sansa?

462
00:33:21,050 --> 00:33:23,517
Not that I know of.

463
00:33:23,552 --> 00:33:26,854
How would you say he died, then?

464
00:33:26,888 --> 00:33:29,056
Choked on his pigeon pie.

465
00:33:30,525 --> 00:33:32,526
So you would blame the bakers?

466
00:33:32,561 --> 00:33:34,945
Or the pigeons.
Just leave me out of it.

467
00:33:34,980 --> 00:33:36,897
<i>(crowd murmuring)</i>

468
00:33:36,948 --> 00:33:40,901
The crown may call
its first witness.

469
00:33:40,903 --> 00:33:43,070
Once we'd got
King Joffrey safely away

470
00:33:43,072 --> 00:33:45,072
from the mob,
the Imp rounded on him.

471
00:33:45,074 --> 00:33:47,408
He slapped the king
across the face

472
00:33:47,459 --> 00:33:49,910
and called him a vicious
idiot and a fool.

473
00:33:49,961 --> 00:33:51,579
(crowd gasps)

474
00:33:51,630 --> 00:33:53,714
<i>It wasn't the first time</i>
<i>the Imp threatened Joffrey.</i>

475
00:33:53,748 --> 00:33:55,633
Right here in this throne room,

476
00:33:55,667 --> 00:33:58,552
he marched up those steps
and called our king a halfwit.

477
00:33:58,587 --> 00:34:00,888
<i>Compared His Grace</i>
<i>to the Mad King</i>

478
00:34:00,922 --> 00:34:02,756
<i>and suggested he'd meet</i>
<i>the same fate.</i>

479
00:34:02,758 --> 00:34:04,592
And when I spoke
in the king's defense,

480
00:34:04,594 --> 00:34:06,927
he threatened to have me killed.

481
00:34:06,978 --> 00:34:08,979
Oh, why don't you tell
them what Joffrey was doing?

482
00:34:09,014 --> 00:34:11,816
- Tywin:<i> Silence.</i>
- Pointing a loaded crossbow at Sansa Stark

483
00:34:11,850 --> 00:34:13,490
while you tore at her
clothes and beat her.

484
00:34:13,518 --> 00:34:16,237
Silence!

485
00:34:16,271 --> 00:34:20,157
You will not speak
unless called upon.

486
00:34:21,493 --> 00:34:23,694
You're dismissed, Ser Meryn.

487
00:34:30,118 --> 00:34:32,786
Basilisk venom,

488
00:34:32,838 --> 00:34:35,339
widow's blood,

489
00:34:35,373 --> 00:34:37,625
wolfsbane,

490
00:34:37,627 --> 00:34:39,844
essence of nightshade,

491
00:34:39,878 --> 00:34:42,763
sweetsleep,

492
00:34:42,797 --> 00:34:44,882
tears of Lys,

493
00:34:44,933 --> 00:34:47,134
demon's dance...

494
00:34:47,185 --> 00:34:49,303
(mumbling)

495
00:34:49,354 --> 00:34:50,888
blind eye--

496
00:34:50,939 --> 00:34:53,140
I think you have made your
point, Grand Maester.

497
00:34:53,142 --> 00:34:54,975
You have a lot of poison
in your store.

498
00:34:55,026 --> 00:34:57,027
<i>Had,</i> Prince Oberyn.

499
00:34:57,062 --> 00:34:59,980
My stores were plundered.

500
00:34:59,982 --> 00:35:01,315
By whom?

501
00:35:01,317 --> 00:35:04,201
Pycelle: <i>By the accused,
Tyrion Lannister,</i>

502
00:35:04,286 --> 00:35:06,654
after he had me
wrongfully imprisoned.

503
00:35:06,656 --> 00:35:09,156
Tywin:
<i>Grand Maester,</i>

504
00:35:09,207 --> 00:35:12,209
you examined
King Joffrey's corpse.

505
00:35:12,244 --> 00:35:16,547
Was it without question
poison that killed him?

506
00:35:16,581 --> 00:35:19,133
Without question.

507
00:35:19,167 --> 00:35:21,418
<i>(crowd murmuring)</i>

508
00:35:21,469 --> 00:35:25,005
This was found on the body
of Dontos Hollard,

509
00:35:25,090 --> 00:35:26,624
the king's fool.

510
00:35:26,658 --> 00:35:30,678
He was last seen
spiriting Sansa Stark,

511
00:35:30,729 --> 00:35:34,481
the wife of the accused,
away from the feast.

512
00:35:34,516 --> 00:35:37,935
She wore this necklace

513
00:35:37,986 --> 00:35:40,187
<i>the day of the wedding.</i>

514
00:35:40,189 --> 00:35:43,490
<i>Residue of a most rare</i>

515
00:35:43,525 --> 00:35:47,494
and terrible poison
was found inside.

516
00:35:47,529 --> 00:35:51,498
Was this one of the poisons
stolen from your store?

517
00:35:51,533 --> 00:35:54,585
It was.
The Strangler.

518
00:35:54,619 --> 00:35:58,455
A poison few in
the Seven Kingdoms possess.

519
00:35:58,490 --> 00:36:03,093
And used to strike down
the most noble child

520
00:36:03,128 --> 00:36:05,930
the gods ever put
on this good earth.

521
00:36:05,964 --> 00:36:08,549
(crowd murmuring)

522
00:36:13,822 --> 00:36:16,140
Cersei:
<i>"I will hurt you for this.</i>

523
00:36:16,191 --> 00:36:18,943
<i>A day will come when you</i>
<i>think you are safe and happy</i>

524
00:36:18,977 --> 00:36:21,395
and your joy will turn
to ashes in your mouth

525
00:36:21,397 --> 00:36:23,564
and you will know
the debt is paid."

526
00:36:23,566 --> 00:36:27,201
Your own brother
said this to you?

527
00:36:28,486 --> 00:36:31,372
Shortly before the Battle
of Blackwater Bay.

528
00:36:31,406 --> 00:36:35,626
I confronted him about his plans
to put Joffrey on the front lines.

529
00:36:38,163 --> 00:36:39,897
As it turned out,
when the attack came,

530
00:36:39,931 --> 00:36:42,249
Joff insisted on remaining
at the battlements.

531
00:36:42,251 --> 00:36:46,170
He believed his presence
would inspire the troops.

532
00:36:47,422 --> 00:36:49,256
Tyrion said,

533
00:36:49,307 --> 00:36:51,976
"And you will know
the debt is paid."

534
00:36:52,010 --> 00:36:54,278
What debt?

535
00:36:55,730 --> 00:36:57,264
I discovered
he'd been keeping whores

536
00:36:57,266 --> 00:36:59,099
in the Tower of the Hand.

537
00:36:59,101 --> 00:37:01,602
I asked him to confine
his salacious acts

538
00:37:01,653 --> 00:37:04,271
<i>to the brothel</i>
<i>where such behavior belongs.</i>

539
00:37:04,273 --> 00:37:06,240
He wasn't pleased.

540
00:37:08,860 --> 00:37:10,995
Thank you, Your Grace,

541
00:37:11,029 --> 00:37:13,330
for the courage
of your testimony.

542
00:37:28,963 --> 00:37:31,382
Mace:<i> Do you remember the
precise nature of this threat?</i>

543
00:37:31,433 --> 00:37:33,967
Varys:
<i>I'm afraid I do, my lord.</i>

544
00:37:34,019 --> 00:37:37,971
<i>He said, "Perhaps you should
speak more softly to me, then.</i>

545
00:37:37,973 --> 00:37:39,890
Monsters are dangerous

546
00:37:39,941 --> 00:37:42,359
and just now kings
are dying like flies."

547
00:37:42,394 --> 00:37:45,696
And he said this to you at a
meeting of the small council?

548
00:37:45,730 --> 00:37:46,980
Yes.

549
00:37:46,982 --> 00:37:50,117
After we received word
of Robb Stark's death.

550
00:37:50,151 --> 00:37:52,403
He didn't seem gladdened
by the news.

551
00:37:52,454 --> 00:37:54,905
Perhaps his marriage
to Sansa Stark

552
00:37:54,956 --> 00:37:57,374
had made him more sympathetic
to the northern cause.

553
00:37:57,409 --> 00:37:59,660
(crowd murmuring)

554
00:38:01,496 --> 00:38:03,831
You're excused, Lord Varys.

555
00:38:05,333 --> 00:38:08,919
Father, may I ask
the witness one question?

556
00:38:14,759 --> 00:38:16,677
One.

557
00:38:19,848 --> 00:38:21,815
You once said

558
00:38:21,850 --> 00:38:25,569
that without me, this city would
have faced certain defeat.

559
00:38:25,603 --> 00:38:29,189
You said the histories
would never mention me,

560
00:38:29,191 --> 00:38:31,859
but you would not forget.

561
00:38:33,194 --> 00:38:35,829
Have you forgotten, Lord Varys?

562
00:38:38,583 --> 00:38:40,451
Sadly, my lord,

563
00:38:40,502 --> 00:38:43,203
I never forget a thing.

564
00:38:52,881 --> 00:38:55,349
We will adjourn for now.

565
00:38:56,718 --> 00:38:59,303
Toll the bells
in an hour's time.

566
00:38:59,354 --> 00:39:01,522
Guard:
<i>Clear the court!</i>

567
00:39:10,398 --> 00:39:13,367
(crowd chatting)

568
00:39:21,376 --> 00:39:23,961
Jaime: <i>You'd condemn
your own son to death?</i>

569
00:39:23,995 --> 00:39:26,163
I've condemned no one.
The trial is not over.

570
00:39:26,214 --> 00:39:27,714
This isn't a trial.
It's a farce.

571
00:39:27,749 --> 00:39:30,050
Cersei has manipulated
everything and you know it.

572
00:39:30,084 --> 00:39:31,502
I know nothing of the sort.

573
00:39:31,553 --> 00:39:34,054
- You've always hated Tyrion.
- He killed his king.

574
00:39:34,088 --> 00:39:35,923
As did I.

575
00:39:35,925 --> 00:39:38,509
Do you know the last order
the Mad King gave me?

576
00:39:38,543 --> 00:39:41,411
<i>To bring him your head.</i>

577
00:39:41,479 --> 00:39:43,931
I saved your life so you
could murder my brother?

578
00:39:43,933 --> 00:39:46,733
- It won't be murder. It'll be justice.
- Justice?

579
00:39:46,768 --> 00:39:49,937
I'm performing my sworn
duty as Hand of the King.

580
00:39:49,939 --> 00:39:53,023
If Tyrion is found guilty, he
will be punished accordingly.

581
00:39:53,074 --> 00:39:55,642
- He'll be executed.
- No, he'll be punished accordingly.

582
00:39:57,946 --> 00:40:01,248
Once you said family
is what lives on.

583
00:40:01,282 --> 00:40:03,283
All that lives on.

584
00:40:03,285 --> 00:40:06,787
You told me about a dynasty
that would last 1,000 years.

585
00:40:06,838 --> 00:40:09,706
What happens to your dynasty
when Tyrion dies?

586
00:40:10,959 --> 00:40:12,626
I'm a Kingsguard,

587
00:40:12,628 --> 00:40:14,545
forbidden by oath
to carry on the family line.

588
00:40:14,596 --> 00:40:16,296
I'm well aware of your oath.

589
00:40:16,298 --> 00:40:18,432
What happens to your name?

590
00:40:18,466 --> 00:40:21,802
Who carries the lion banner
into future battles?

591
00:40:21,853 --> 00:40:23,804
Your nephews?

592
00:40:23,855 --> 00:40:25,439
Lancel Lannister?

593
00:40:25,473 --> 00:40:27,691
Others whose names
I don't even remember?

594
00:40:27,725 --> 00:40:29,393
What happens to my dynasty

595
00:40:29,444 --> 00:40:31,662
if I spare the life
of my grandson's killer?

596
00:40:31,696 --> 00:40:34,281
It survives through me.

597
00:40:36,818 --> 00:40:38,652
I'll leave the Kingsguard.

598
00:40:38,654 --> 00:40:41,488
I'll take my place
as your son and heir

599
00:40:41,490 --> 00:40:44,374
if you let Tyrion live.

600
00:40:44,409 --> 00:40:45,876
Done.

601
00:40:47,412 --> 00:40:50,998
When the testimony's concluded
and a guilty verdict rendered,

602
00:40:51,000 --> 00:40:52,749
Tyrion will be given
the chance to speak.

603
00:40:52,800 --> 00:40:56,803
He'll plead for mercy. I'll allow
him to join the Night's Watch.

604
00:40:58,556 --> 00:41:01,341
<i>In three days' time, he'll
depart for Castle Black</i>

605
00:41:01,392 --> 00:41:03,060
<i>and live out his days</i>
<i>at the Wall.</i>

606
00:41:03,094 --> 00:41:05,679
<i>You'll remove your</i>
<i>white cloak immediately.</i>

607
00:41:05,681 --> 00:41:07,681
<i>You will leave King's Landing</i>

608
00:41:07,732 --> 00:41:09,933
<i>to assume your rightful</i>
<i>place at Casterly Rock.</i>

609
00:41:09,984 --> 00:41:12,402
You will marry a suitable woman

610
00:41:12,437 --> 00:41:15,489
and father children
named Lannister.

611
00:41:15,523 --> 00:41:19,409
And you'll never turn your
back on your family again.

612
00:41:24,532 --> 00:41:26,033
You have my word.

613
00:41:26,035 --> 00:41:27,868
And you have mine.

614
00:41:31,256 --> 00:41:34,841
<i>(bell tolling)</i>

615
00:41:51,976 --> 00:41:54,111
Not going well, is it?

616
00:41:54,145 --> 00:41:56,613
- You're going to be found guilty.
- Oh, you think so?

617
00:41:56,648 --> 00:41:59,399
When you are, you need to
enter a formal plea for mercy

618
00:41:59,450 --> 00:42:01,702
and ask to be sent to the Wall.

619
00:42:01,736 --> 00:42:03,704
Father's agreed to it.

620
00:42:03,738 --> 00:42:06,456
He'll spare your life and allow
you to join the Night's Watch.

621
00:42:06,491 --> 00:42:09,126
Ned Stark was promised
the same thing

622
00:42:09,160 --> 00:42:11,128
<i>and we both know</i>
<i>how that turned out.</i>

623
00:42:11,162 --> 00:42:13,080
Jaime: <i>Father is not Joffrey.
He'll keep his word.</i>

624
00:42:13,082 --> 00:42:14,915
How do you know?

625
00:42:18,720 --> 00:42:20,637
Do you trust me?

626
00:42:23,591 --> 00:42:26,176
Keep your mouth shut.
No more outbursts.

627
00:42:26,227 --> 00:42:28,261
This will all be over soon.

628
00:42:44,195 --> 00:42:47,114
The crown may call
its next witness.

629
00:42:51,586 --> 00:42:54,287
<i>(footsteps)</i>

630
00:43:21,599 --> 00:43:23,650
State your name.

631
00:43:23,652 --> 00:43:25,652
Shae.

632
00:43:25,654 --> 00:43:27,871
Do you swear by all the gods

633
00:43:27,905 --> 00:43:30,457
that your testimony
will be true and honest?

634
00:43:30,491 --> 00:43:33,326
I swear it.

635
00:43:33,378 --> 00:43:35,162
Do you know this man?

636
00:43:42,837 --> 00:43:46,073
Yes.
Tyrion Lannister.

637
00:43:46,107 --> 00:43:47,924
How do you know him?

638
00:43:47,975 --> 00:43:51,228
I was handmaiden
to his wife Lady Sansa.

639
00:43:51,262 --> 00:43:55,348
This man stands accused
of murdering King Joffrey.

640
00:43:55,350 --> 00:43:57,567
What do you know of this?

641
00:43:59,187 --> 00:44:01,488
I know that he's guilty.

642
00:44:01,522 --> 00:44:02,773
(crowd gasps)

643
00:44:02,824 --> 00:44:04,908
He and Sansa
planned it together.

644
00:44:04,942 --> 00:44:06,193
(crowd murmuring)

645
00:44:06,195 --> 00:44:08,695
Tywin:
<i>Silence!</i>

646
00:44:08,746 --> 00:44:11,248
<i>Continue.</i>

647
00:44:11,282 --> 00:44:14,201
Shae: <i>She wanted revenge
for her father,</i>

648
00:44:14,203 --> 00:44:15,702
<i>her mother, her brother.</i>

649
00:44:15,753 --> 00:44:18,538
<i>She blamed their deaths</i>
<i>on the king.</i>

650
00:44:18,540 --> 00:44:20,957
<i>Tyrion was happy to help.</i>

651
00:44:21,008 --> 00:44:24,628
He hated Joffrey.
He hated the queen.

652
00:44:24,679 --> 00:44:27,697
He hated you, my lord.

653
00:44:27,732 --> 00:44:30,884
He stole poison from
the Grand Maester's chamber

654
00:44:30,935 --> 00:44:32,786
to put in Joffrey's wine.

655
00:44:32,820 --> 00:44:35,355
Oberyn: <i>How could you
possibly know all this?</i>

656
00:44:35,389 --> 00:44:39,192
Why would he reveal
such plans to his wife's maid?

657
00:44:40,812 --> 00:44:43,246
I wasn't just her maid.

658
00:44:46,067 --> 00:44:47,734
I was his whore.

659
00:44:47,736 --> 00:44:50,737
- (crowd murmuring)
- Mace:<i> I beg your pardon?</i>

660
00:44:50,739 --> 00:44:52,405
You said you were his...

661
00:44:52,457 --> 00:44:54,291
His whore.

662
00:44:57,161 --> 00:44:59,629
How did you come
to be in his service?

663
00:44:59,664 --> 00:45:00,964
He stole me.

664
00:45:00,998 --> 00:45:02,499
I was with another man,

665
00:45:02,550 --> 00:45:04,084
a knight in
your lordship's army.

666
00:45:04,086 --> 00:45:06,636
But when Tyrion arrived
at the camp,

667
00:45:06,721 --> 00:45:08,889
he sent one of his cutthroats
into our tent.

668
00:45:08,923 --> 00:45:13,426
<i>He broke the knight's arm</i>
<i>and brought me to Lord Tyrion.</i>

669
00:45:13,428 --> 00:45:15,929
"You belong to me now," he said.

670
00:45:15,980 --> 00:45:19,933
"I want you to fuck me like it's
my last night in this world."

671
00:45:19,935 --> 00:45:22,652
(crowd laughs)

672
00:45:22,687 --> 00:45:25,405
Silence.
Silence!

673
00:45:25,439 --> 00:45:26,656
<i>(laughing stops)</i>

674
00:45:26,691 --> 00:45:28,325
Oberyn:
And did you?

675
00:45:28,359 --> 00:45:30,861
Did I what?

676
00:45:30,912 --> 00:45:33,580
Fuck him like it was
his last night in this world.

677
00:45:33,614 --> 00:45:35,582
<i>(crowd laughs)</i>

678
00:45:35,616 --> 00:45:38,084
I did everything he wanted.

679
00:45:38,119 --> 00:45:40,170
Whatever he told me
to do to him.

680
00:45:40,290 --> 00:45:42,066
Whatever he felt like doing to me.

681
00:45:42,305 --> 00:45:44,187
<i>I kissed him where he wanted.</i>

682
00:45:44,307 --> 00:45:45,709
<i>I licked him where he wanted.</i>

683
00:45:45,829 --> 00:45:49,144
<i>I let him put himself</i>
<i>where he wanted.</i>

684
00:45:49,195 --> 00:45:51,113
I was his property.

685
00:45:51,147 --> 00:45:53,365
I would wait in his chambers
for hours

686
00:45:53,399 --> 00:45:55,234
so he could use me
when he was bored.

687
00:45:55,285 --> 00:45:59,204
He ordered me to call
him "my lion," so I did.

688
00:45:59,239 --> 00:46:02,908
I took his face
in my hands and said,

689
00:46:02,959 --> 00:46:05,994
"I am yours
and you are mine."

690
00:46:05,996 --> 00:46:08,830
(crowd murmuring)

691
00:46:09,716 --> 00:46:11,250
Shae.

692
00:46:14,470 --> 00:46:16,505
Please don't.

693
00:46:18,725 --> 00:46:21,059
I am a whore.

694
00:46:21,094 --> 00:46:23,679
<i>Remember?</i>

695
00:46:25,648 --> 00:46:27,683
That was before
he married Sansa.

696
00:46:27,685 --> 00:46:30,352
After that,
all he wanted was her.

697
00:46:30,354 --> 00:46:32,938
But she wouldn't let him
into her bed.

698
00:46:34,407 --> 00:46:37,192
So he promised to kill
King Joffrey for her.

699
00:46:37,194 --> 00:46:39,194
<i>(crowd clamoring)</i>

700
00:46:48,421 --> 00:46:51,039
I wish to confess.

701
00:46:52,258 --> 00:46:55,543
I wish...
to confess.

702
00:46:55,594 --> 00:46:57,679
<i>(crowd quiets)</i>

703
00:46:57,713 --> 00:47:00,215
You wish to confess?

704
00:47:02,101 --> 00:47:03,718
I saved you.

705
00:47:05,471 --> 00:47:07,772
I saved this city

706
00:47:07,807 --> 00:47:11,309
and all your worthless lives.

707
00:47:11,360 --> 00:47:14,863
I should have let
Stannis kill you all.

708
00:47:14,897 --> 00:47:17,532
- (clamoring)
- Tywin: Tyrion.

709
00:47:20,403 --> 00:47:22,904
Tywin:
Do you wish to confess?

710
00:47:26,459 --> 00:47:29,044
Yes, Father.

711
00:47:29,078 --> 00:47:31,579
I'm guilty.

712
00:47:31,614 --> 00:47:34,582
Guilty. Is that what
you want to hear?

713
00:47:34,584 --> 00:47:36,718
